Because earlier loops embody less computation, recurrence inherently supplies aligned weak-and-strong prediction pairs without auxiliary models or external training. We introduce LoopCD, a training-free contrastive decoding framework that guides token select","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-02","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2610_02185.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"Looped Transformer 在解码时只使用最终循环的输出，浪费了前面循环天然提供的弱-强预测对，导致推理质量没有吃满已有计算。","importance":"这是推理侧方法创新。它不改训练、不加辅助模型，直接把循环结构里的中间表征变成 contrastive decoding 信号，对 recurrent/looped LLM 的 serving 和 test-time compute 设计有参考价值。","technical_approach":"提出 LoopCD，一个 training-free 的对比解码框架。核心做法是用较早循环的预测作为“弱模型”，最终循环作为“强模型”，在 token 选择时做对比。作者给出两种实现：LoopCD-Logits 在 logit 空间对比，需要额外一次输出头前向；LoopCD-Hidden 在 hidden state 空间构造对比信号，几乎没有额外输出开销。方法利用 looped Transformer 各循环对同一 next token 的对齐性质，不需要外部 draft model 或额外训练。","experiments":"覆盖四个 looped Transformer 家族。摘要给出的代表结果包括：Ouro-2.6B-Thinking 在 AIME 2024 pass@1 从 61.88% 提升到 73.33%；Huginn 在 HumanEval pass@1 从 22.56% 提升到 31.71%。重点是这些提升发生在 full recurrent depth 下，说明收益不是简单来自多跑几步，而是来自更好的解码准则。论文未在摘要中给出更系统的延迟、吞吐和不同 loop 深度的完整表格，读正文时应重点核对这部分。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Standard decoding for looped Transformers uses only the final recurrent pass and discards earlier aligned predictions, leaving useful compute on the table.","importance":"This is an inference-time method contribution. It turns the recurrent structure itself into a source of weak-vs-strong contrastive signals, which is relevant for serving and test-time compute design in recurrent or looped LLMs.","technical_approach":"The paper proposes LoopCD, a training-free contrastive decoding method. An earlier loop acts as the weak predictor and the final loop as the strong predictor for the same next token. Two variants are introduced: LoopCD-Logits contrasts in logit space with one extra output pass, while LoopCD-Hidden works in hidden-state space with near-zero output overhead. No auxiliary draft model or extra training is required.","experiments":"The method is tested on four looped Transformer families. curation for synthetic data mixtures"},"claim":{"zh":"在固定 token 预算下，合成数据的训练收益主要由“样本组合的互补性/覆盖度”决定而非单样本质量；用组级效用信号（显式建模样本间交互）筛选得到的合成子集，会在相同训练步数下同时降低验证集 loss 并提高 post-train 的 Pass@k 上限。","en":"Under a fixed token budget, synthetic-data gains are dominated by mixture complementarity/coverage rather than per-sample quality; selecting synthetic subsets with group-level utility (explicit interaction-aware scoring) will reduce val loss and raise post-train Pass@k ceilings at matched steps."},"why_scales":{"zh":"组级信号优化的是“覆盖与冗余”的统计结构（有效支持集大小、n-gram/技能覆盖、错误相关性），这是随规模放大仍然存在的瓶颈：大模型更容易把冗余样本学到极致，从而更快进入边际收益递减；因此交互感知的去冗余/补覆盖会在更大算力下更显著，而不是被抹平。它也更不易秩反转：单样本打分往往依赖小模型的局部可学性特征（spurious easiness），跨尺度会变；组级目标直接约束“组合后的分布形状”，对模型容量更不敏感。","en":"Group-level signals optimize statistical structure (coverage vs redundancy, effective support size, skill/ngram coverage, error correlation) that remains a bottleneck at scale: larger models fit redundancy more completely, hitting diminishing returns sooner, so de-redundancy and complementary coverage matter more, not less. This is less prone to rank reversal because per-sample scoring often exploits small-model-specific easiness features, while group-level objectives constrain the mixture distribution shape, which is less capacity-dependent."},"plan":{"goal":{"zh":"验证“组级策展 > 单样本策展/随机采样”在预训练 loss 与 post-train 可训练性（Pass@k 斜率/上限）上的一致优势，并测量其对秩反转的鲁棒性。","en":"Validate that group-level curation beats per-sample curation/random sampling on both pretrain loss and post-trainability (Pass@k slope/ceiling), and measure robustness to rank reversal."},"data_sources":{"zh":"Proxy ladder：150M、350M、1.3B 三档 decoder-only（同 tokenizer/上下文长度），Iso-FLOPs 训练到相同 tokens（如每档 30B tokens；或按 Chinchilla 比例缩放）。数据：真实 web/text 作为 anchor（例如 70%），合成指令/推理/代码作为 variable（30%）。合成池至少 5–10 倍于最终使用量，确保存在强冗余与组合效应。","en":"Proxy ladder: 150M, 350M, 1.3B decoder-only (same tokenizer/ctx), trained Iso-FLOPs to matched tokens (e.g., 30B each, or Chinchilla-scaled). Data: real web/text as anchor (e.g., 70%), synthetic instruction/reasoning/code as variable (30%). Synthetic pool 5–10× larger than final usage to ensure redundancy and interaction effects."},"steps":[{"zh":"步骤 1：构造三种合成子集（token 数严格相同）：A) 随机采样；B) 单样本信号（如 teacher logprob / reward / influence proxy）top-k；C) 组级信号：以 batch/group 为单位优化“覆盖-冗余”目标（可实现为：最大化 group 内 embedding 多样性 + 约束难度分布 + 去重；或直接复现论文的 group-utility 估计器）。三者与同一真实数据 anchor 混合，训练到相同 tokens。","en":"Step 1: Build three synthetic subsets with identical token counts: A) random; B) per-sample scoring (e.g., teacher logprob/reward/influence proxy) top-k; C) group-level signal optimizing coverage–redundancy at group granularity (e.g., maximize within-group embedding diversity + constrain difficulty histogram + dedup; or reproduce the paper’s group-utility estimator). Mix each with the same real-data anchor and train to matched tokens."},{"zh":"步骤 2：在每个 base checkpoint 上做同配方 post-train probe（轻量 SFT 或 RLVR 小预算），并做跨尺度一致性检查：比较 150M→350M→1.3B 上三种策展的排序是否稳定；同时做“合成占比 sweep”（10/30/50%）验证边界。","en":"Step 2: Run identical post-train probes (light SFT or small-budget RLVR) on each base checkpoint and check cross-scale consistency: whether the ranking of A/B/C is stable from 150M→350M→1.3B; also sweep synthetic ratio (10/30/50%) to map boundaries."}],"eval_signal":{"zh":"预训练：分域验证集 cross-entropy（真实域、合成域、以及 held-out reasoning/code 域），以及重复/覆盖代理（unique n-grams、topic/skill coverage、样本间 embedding 相似度分布）。后训练：固定预算下 Pass@k-Compute 曲线斜率（如 pass@1/4/16）与多数投票增益（反映错误相关性）。","en":"Pretrain: domain-sliced val cross-entropy (real, synthetic, held-out reasoning/code), plus redundancy/coverage proxies (unique n-grams, topic/skill coverage, pairwise embedding similarity distribution). Post-train: Pass@k vs compute slope (pass@1/4/16) under fixed budget and majority-vote gain (proxy for error correlation)."},"success_criteria":{"zh":"在至少两档尺度（≥350M）上同时满足：C 相对 B 在真实域 val loss 下降 ≥0.3% 且在 held-out reasoning/code 域下降 ≥0.7%；并且 post-train probe 的 pass@16 提升 ≥2.0 绝对点或 Pass@k 斜率提升 ≥10%。若 C 仅在小模型赢、到 1.3B 排名反转，则证伪“可外推”。","en":"On at least two scales (≥350M), C must beat B with ≥0.3% lower real-domain val loss and ≥0.7% lower held-out reasoning/code loss, and improve post-train pass@16 by ≥2.0 absolute points or increase Pass@k slope by ≥10%. If C wins only on the smallest model but reverses by 1.3B, the scalability claim is falsified."},"compute_tier":"medium"},"risk":{"zh":"组级目标若退化为“只追多样性”可能引入分布漂移（coverage 变广但偏离目标任务），导致小模型看似更好、大模型反而更差；需要用 anchor 真实数据与难度直方图约束，避免把策展变成 domain shift。","en":"If the group objective collapses into diversity-only, it can induce distribution shift (broader but off-target coverage), yielding apparent gains on small models but worse large-model behavior; must constrain with real-data anchors and difficulty histograms to avoid domain shift."},"source_paper_ids":["2610.00779"],"scaling_confidence":0.78},{"pillar":"scaling_ladder","title":{"zh":"混合SSM架构的LR零差迁移","en":"Near-zero LR transfer for hybrid Transformer-SSM"},"claim":{"zh":"在工程常用的 fixed-state-size Mamba/SSM 混合块中，即使 µP coordinate-check 失败，只要满足全局 update-to-weight 比例近似不变，学习率与优化超参可以从 150M 级几乎无调参迁移到 1B+，且不会出现跨尺度秩反转。","en":"For practical fixed-state-size Mamba/SSM hybrid blocks, even when µP coordinate-check fails, if global update-to-weight ratios are approximately invariant, LR/optimizer hyperparameters transfer from ~150M to 1B+ with minimal retuning and without rank reversal."},"why_scales":{"zh":"LR 迁移是否成立，本质取决于训练动力学的尺度不变性：参数更新幅度相对权重幅度（以及激活/梯度统计）是否随宽度/深度保持稳定。混合 SSM 的“局部不满足 µP 条件”不必然破坏全局不变性；如果主要误差来自局部块的动态而非全网更新尺度，那么在更大模型上这种不变性反而更容易通过宽度平均效应稳定下来，从而降低秩反转风险。该结论一旦在宽/深双轴 ladder 上成立，外推到更大规模通常比“某个 trick 提升一点点”更稳。","en":"LR transfer hinges on scale-invariant training dynamics: whether update magnitudes relative to weights (and activation/gradient statistics) stay stable across width/depth. Hybrid SSM blocks can violate local µP assumptions without breaking global invariance; if the dominant factor is global update scaling rather than local block idiosyncrasies, larger models can stabilize this via width-averaging, reducing rank-reversal risk. If it holds on a width/depth ladder, it typically extrapolates more reliably than small additive tricks."},"plan":{"goal":{"zh":"用最小成本验证“近零 LR transfer gap”是否在你们的混合 Transformer-SSM 配方中成立，并建立可用于大模型的超参迁移与秩反转诊断面板。","en":"Validate near-zero LR transfer gap in your hybrid Transformer-SSM recipe at minimal cost, and build a hyperparameter-transfer + rank-reversal diagnostic panel for scaling."},"data_sources":{"zh":"模型 ladder（Iso-FLOPs）：150M、350M、1.3B；每档做宽度×深度两维 sweep（例如 width {384, 768, 1152} × depth {12, 24, 36}，保持总参数落在目标档位附近）。数据用单一干净文本分布（如 OpenWebText/内部 web mix 的固定子集），训练到相同 tokens（如 10B）以突出优化差异而非数据差异。","en":"Model ladder (Iso-FLOPs): 150M, 350M, 1.3B; run a 2D width×depth sweep per tier (e.g., width {384, 768, 1152} × depth {12, 24, 36}, keeping params near tier target). Use a single clean text distribution (fixed subset of OWT/internal web mix) and train to matched tokens (e.g., 10B) to isolate optimization effects."},"steps":[{"zh":"步骤 1：在 150M 上用标准搜索找到 baseline 超参（AdamW/β、wd、lr、warmup、clip、dropout），并记录训练动力学统计：每层 update/weight 比、梯度范数分布、激活 RMS、loss spike 频率。","en":"Step 1: Tune baseline hyperparams on 150M (AdamW/β, wd, lr, warmup, clip, dropout) and log dynamics: per-layer update/weight ratios, grad-norm distribution, activation RMS, loss-spike frequency."},{"zh":"步骤 2：把同一套超参直接迁移到 350M 与 1.3B（不改 lr），只允许按 token 数等比例调整 warmup steps（可选对照：完全不调 warmup）。对比“重新搜索 lr”的 oracle 结果，计算 LR transfer gap，并检查不同宽/深组合是否出现秩反转。","en":"Step 2: Transfer the exact hyperparams to 350M and 1.3B (no lr change), only optionally scaling warmup steps with token count (and a control with no warmup change). Compare against an oracle lr retune, compute LR transfer gap, and check for rank reversal across width/depth variants."}],"eval_signal":{"zh":"核心：在固定 tokens 下的验证集 loss（或 bits-per-byte）与收敛速度；LR transfer gap = (loss_transfer - loss_oracle)/loss_oracle。诊断：update/weight 比在尺度间的漂移（均值与分位数）、梯度噪声尺度 proxy、以及 loss spike 率。","en":"Core: validation loss (or bits-per-byte) at fixed tokens and convergence speed; LR transfer gap = (loss_transfer - loss_oracle)/loss_oracle. Diagnostics: cross-scale drift of update/weight ratios (mean/quantiles), gradient-noise-scale proxy, and loss-spike rate."},"success_criteria":{"zh":"在 350M 与 1.3B 上，transfer 超参的端点 loss 距离 oracle ≤0.5%（相对），且在宽/深 sweep 中排名与 oracle 的 Spearman ρ ≥0.9（无明显秩反转）。若 gap >1.5% 或 ρ <0.7，则说明该架构/实现不满足可迁移条件，需要回到 µP/参数化或局部块稳定性上排查。","en":"On 350M and 1.3B, transferred hyperparams must achieve endpoint loss within ≤0.5% (relative) of oracle, and width/depth ranking must match oracle with Spearman ρ ≥0.9 (no meaningful rank reversal). If gap >1.5% or ρ <0.7, transferability is not met; investigate µP/parameterization or local-block stability."},"compute_tier":"small"},"risk":{"zh":"实现细节（SSM kernel、state init、混合残差比例、norm 位置）可能让“局部动态”主导，从而在更大宽度下出现训练不稳或不同深度最优点漂移，导致秩反转；需要把日志细化到 block 级并做最小化改动的稳定性对照（如仅替换 norm/残差缩放）。","en":"Implementation details (SSM kernel, state init, residual mixing ratio, norm placement) can make local dynamics dominate, causing instability at larger widths or shifting optimal depth, leading to rank reversal; require block-level logging and minimal-change stability controls (e.g., norm/residual scaling only)."},"source_paper_ids":["2610.01172"],"scaling_confidence":0.81}]},"dates":"$9:0:props:dates","currentDate":"2026-10-05"}]}]}]
